--- entity_google_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
review_id: 2505
entity_slug: china-dragon
google_review_name: places/ChIJu5pMMt8EmogRZhZkp4iCBAc/reviews/Ci9DQUlRQUNvZENodHljRjlvT21kRGFUTm1kRmhaVmtGaldUTkpVM2R1ZEdsM2RVRRAB
rating: 5
review_text: Having lived many years in Hong Kong, I’ve become pretty particular about Chinese food. I think a lot of places here lean toward the Americanized version, and while there’s nothing wrong with that, it’s just not always what I’m craving. I tend to miss the depth and balance of flavors I was used to.
Last weekend my husband had a serious hankering for Chinese food, so we decided to try China Dragon, Gulf Shores.
When we walked in, Ren, Lin and Quay were so warm and hospitable. Although they were trying to learn English, they were able to communicate  via google translate and it worked  well.
And the food? So good. The portions easily lasted us two meals. The hot and sour soup honestly reminded me of what I used to have in Hong Kong. I asked if they could make our dishes more authentic, and they were happy to accomodate. Everything was flavorful without being overly salty.
Don’t let the 3.6 reviews stop you. If you’re looking for authentic Chinese food around here, this place is the one to go to!!

BTW Gong Xi Fa Cai Ren, Lin and Quay!!!
language_code: en
author_name: Adwiti Meditations for You

--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: d2022142-6ca6-4ca8-9c4a-36467f442128
entity_slug: china-dragon
reviewer_name: Adwiti Meditations for You
rating: 5
body: Having lived many years in Hong Kong, I’ve become pretty particular about Chinese food. I think a lot of places here lean toward the Americanized version, and while there’s nothing wrong with that, it’s just not always what I’m craving. I tend to miss the depth and balance of flavors I was used to.
Last weekend my husband had a serious hankering for Chinese food, so we decided to try China Dragon, Gulf Shores.
When we walked in, Ren, Lin and Quay were so warm and hospitable. Although they were trying to learn English, they were able to communicate  via google translate and it worked  well.
And the food? So good. The portions easily lasted us two meals. The hot and sour soup honestly reminded me of what I used to have in Hong Kong. I asked if they could make our dishes more authentic, and they were happy to accomodate. Everything was flavorful without being overly salty.
Don’t let the 3.6 reviews stop you. If you’re looking for authentic Chinese food around here, this place is the one to go to!!

BTW Gong Xi Fa Cai Ren, Lin and Quay!!!

helpful_count: 0
approved: true
